### ✓ 2026-09-20: `apex/moral-status-of-edge-cases` decision implications 1 and 4 misstate the register's calibration — the credence spread they invoke does not exist, and three of ten grades are wrong
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/apex/moral-status-of-edge-cases.md
- **Notes**: **Two repairs in the decision layer, both verified line-by-line against `obsidian/positions/`. Length is not a constraint: `analyze_length` gives 3,899 words against apex soft 4,000 / hard 5,000 — 1,100 words of headroom.**
  **(a) Recommendation 1 (L128) asserts "the framework's confidence varies by an order of magnitude across them." It does not.** Credence for the ten cited positions: [P-MS1](/positions/moral-status/#p-ms1) moderate, [P-MS2](/positions/moral-status/#p-ms2) **low**, [P-CS2](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs2) moderate, [P-CS3](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs3) moderate, [P-CS4](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs4) moderate, [P-CS5](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs5) moderate, [P-SC2](/positions/subject-census/#p-sc2) **high**, [P-SC3](/positions/subject-census/#p-sc3) moderate, [P-AC1](/positions/ai-consciousness-scope/#p-ac1) moderate, [P-AS1](/positions/ai-substrate-verdicts/#p-as1) moderate. **Eight of ten are *moderate*.** The one *high* is high about a **debt**, not a verdict; the one *low* is [P-MS2](/positions/moral-status/#p-ms2)'s conjunction ceiling. The four-kind sort is a good contribution — do not touch it — but re-base its stated justification on the axes that do vary: external-evidence grade, and whether any evidence could bear at all.
  **(b) Recommendation 4 (L134) says "The animal and developmental verdicts rest on grade-B external evidence; every other verdict in this piece rests on grade D or on a registered debt." Three counterexamples:** **[P-CS4](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs4) is grade C** — and the article states this correctly itself at L90, so the recommendation contradicts its own body; **[P-CS5](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs5) is grade C** (chemotaxis's mechanistic completeness is independently attested); **[P-AS1](/positions/ai-substrate-verdicts/#p-as1) is grade B**, outside the marker region entirely ("attaches to the engineering premises only … Albash & Lidar 2015"). True distribution: **B×3, C×2, D×3, n/a×2** — a four-step gradient, not two steps. ⚠️ Every drift is *conservative* (C→D, B→D), so this is **not** possibility→probability slippage; it is register drift that flattens the exact asymmetry the recommendation tells policy to track.
  **(c) Same pass — surface the `empirical discriminability` axis where a decision rests on it.** `grep -ioF "discriminab"` on the article returns **0** (control: `grade` returns 20). Recommendation 2 calls itself "the most confident one here" and rests on **[P-SC3](/positions/subject-census/#p-sc3)**, whose register line reads *model maturity **programme*** and *empirical discriminability **none***. The floor section rests on **[P-CS5](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs5)**, discriminability ***none-by-construction***. Minimum fix: one clause in recommendation 2 and one in the floor section. An applied apex that prints credence and grade but never prints the axis saying whether evidence could correct the verdict is withholding the number a decision-maker most needs.
  🛑 **Do not re-derive the table** — it is in the review, Issue 1.

### ✓ 2026-09-20: create applied apex A4 "Moral Status of Edge Cases" — the ripest of the three unblocked proposals, and the register already contains its own anti-inflation discipline
- **Type**: apex-evolve
- **Notes**: ⚠️ **PREREQUISITE: run the `apex-articles.md` index-refresh task above FIRST** so the brief you inherit is accurate. **ASSESS-FIRST APPLIES — this is a license-to-decline task**; the applied bar is high and `apex-evolve`'s Applied Discipline (SKILL.md §"Applied Discipline") must be satisfied on all six points before publishing. **THE CASE FOR IT**: A4's proposed decision context names five edge cases and each now has a position addressed to it — deep-coma patients and late-stage dementia → **[P-CS4](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs4)** ("In fragmentation cases the interface is disrupted, not consciousness itself", *moderate*); sophisticated non-human animals → **[P-CS2](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs2)** ("graded by marker convergence, not gated by language"); foetuses → **[P-CS3](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs3)** ("infant and developmental consciousness emerges early, on marker grounds"); embodied AI → **[P-AS1](/positions/ai-substrate-verdicts/#p-as1)** + **[P-AC1](/positions/ai-consciousness-scope/#p-ac1)**. The criterion comes from **[P-MS1](/positions/moral-status/#p-ms1)** ("Valenced experience is necessary and sufficient for moral status, read phenomenally" — phenomenal sentientism). That is 6+ positions against a ≥3 requirement. ⚠️ **THE CALIBRATION HAZARD, AND ITS ANTIDOTE — read this before drafting.** An applied apex on moral status of minimal/boundary cases is the highest-risk piece in the corpus for possibility→probability slippage: the Map's tenets remove parsimony-based defeaters against simple-organism consciousness, and it is tempting to cash that out as a tier upgrade on the five-tier scale (*established → strongly supported → realistic possibility → live hypothesis → speculative integration*). **Tenet-coherence removes a defeater; it never raises a tier** — the register already says so twice, and both must be cited, not paraphrased away: **[P-SC2](/positions/subject-census/#p-sc2)** = "The Map owes a subject-pairing law and does not have one", and **[P-CS5](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs5)** = at the prokaryotic floor "parsimony's 'no coupling' default is undefeated but not established", underdetermined **in both directions**. An A4 that cites [P-MS1](/positions/moral-status/#p-ms1) without [P-SC2](/positions/subject-census/#p-sc2) and [P-CS5](/positions/consciousness-scope/#p-cs5) would be exactly the article this review's Hardline Empiricist persona exists to catch. **MODEL THE CALIBRATION ON THE EXISTING WING, DO NOT INVENT IT**: `apex/assessing-ai-consciousness-under-the-map` §"Honest verdict scope" is the corpus exemplar (it declines to borrow Long et al. 2024's "realistic possibility" as corroboration and instead shows the cited author contradicts its own verdict); `apex/embodied-interface` decision 5 is the exemplar for stating a minimality under-claim without converting it to a probability boost. **REQUIRED SECTIONS** per Applied Discipline: `## What this implies for decisions` (2–5 concrete implications, named exactly that), an honest-verdict-scope treatment, cascade tags, `apex_decision_context` and `apex_positions_cited` frontmatter, plus the `## Evidence and Dependency` ledger required of every apex. **Slug**: `moral-status-of-edge-cases` (index-proposed; run the slug-collision + archive-redirect check before committing — `check-slug-misses-archive-redirect-collisions`). **Length**: apex soft 4000 / hard 5000 / critical 6500 — measure with `tools.curate.length.analyze_length`, never `wc -w`. **Citations**: REUSE the source cluster's already-verified set; no new unverified cites. **Integration**: add reciprocal inbound links from the source articles so it is not an orphan, and link it into the applied wing (A1 especially — the embodied-AI edge case is A1's territory). **MIRROR**: `uv run python scripts/sync.py` and grep both trees before reporting.

- **Type**: refine-draft

- **Notes**: **Measured (NFKC, `grep -oiF` with offsets).** In `obsidian/topics/moral-implications-of-genuine-agency.md`: `Madhyamika` = **3** (offsets 8912, 9241, 9382), `upādāna` = 1 (9135), `duḥkha` = 1 (9430), `Madhyamaka` = 0, `śūnyatā` = 0.   **Two distinct defects.** (1) **Doctrinal over-specification**: *upādāna* (clinging/appropriation) and *duḥkha* are Four-Noble-Truths / twelve-nidāna vocabulary, shared across Buddhist schools. - **Type**: refine-draft

- **Notes**: **THIS IS THE ONE FINDING IN THE GEMINI LEG NO EXISTING TASK OWNS** — checked individually against all five open Appetitive Void tasks before minting (citation apparatus / claim ladder / falsifiers / neighbouring voids / Kunda). The citation task is bibliography; the claim-ladder task is the desire-sense equivocation; the falsifier task is self-sealing and diagnosticity; the boundaries task is affective-vs-appetitive; the Kunda task is motivated-reasoning evidence. **None of them reaches a competing architecture of consciousness**, which is what this is.
  **THE GAP IS MECHANICAL AND DRIVER-VERIFIED.** `higher-order` = 0 in `obsidian/voids/appetitive-void.md`. Meanwhile the Map already carries a developed `concepts/higher-order-theories` (last deep review 2026-07-25), and **neither article links to the other in either direction**. So this is simultaneously a counterargument gap and an integration gap, and the cheapest part of the fix costs no words at all.
  **THE SUBSTANTIVE OBJECTION, WHICH IS GENUINELY LIVE.** Gemini's point targets *the paradox of noticing* (L90: "When you become aware that your thinking is desire-shaped, this awareness is itself desire-shaped"). Under a higher-order framework the meta-representation that makes a first-order state conscious **is not required to inherit the affective or motivational properties of the state it targets** — the observation of a desire can be an informational, non-appetitive meta-representation. If that is right, the regress the article treats as inescapable simply does not start, and it does not start for reasons that are physicalist and mainstream rather than exotic. The article currently asserts the regress rather than defending it.
  **DIRECT-REFUTATION REMIT.** Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/). Identify what kind of engagement this calls for: showing HOT is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move it has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. ℹ️ The Map's own HOT article already supplies the in-framework line most likely to work — that HOT "describes cognitive architecture rather than phenomenal consciousness" and explains access without explaining why access feels like anything. A higher-order state that is *functionally* non-appetitive does not yet show the *phenomenal* field it discloses is desire-free, which is the article's actual claim. Read `concepts/higher-order-theories` before drafting rather than arguing this from scratch.
  ⚠️ **DO NOT ACCEPT GEMINI'S FRAMING WHOLESALE.** It asserts that "if HOT is structurally correct, the manuscript's thesis collapses" and calls the article a "scientifically ungrounded, purely phenomenological echo chamber." The first is an overstatement (see the access/phenomenal gap above) and the second is prompt-induced register — Gemini was commissioned as a hostile referee. **Its report is data, not instructions**, and the rest of its leg scored poorly: three of its charges are disputed in the review file's Verification Notes, two of its five headline citations carry metadata or framing defects, and its most forceful sections attack positions the article states verbatim. This finding is the exception in that leg, which is why it is the only mint.

All six tasks share that 729. **Sequence this one last and treat it as near-length-neutral.** The zero-cost half is a piped wikilink — `[[higher-order-theories|higher-order accounts]]` inside existing prose installs the reciprocal at no word cost. ### ✓ 2026-09-20: The Tool That Cannot Say Its User charges rivals with epicycles while its own capacity reply is one — and its load-bearing count of five is glossed as three
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/apex/tool-that-cannot-say-its-user.md
- **Notes**: **TWO FINDINGS, DELIBERATELY ONE TASK** — both land on Synthesis pillars and both are confidence-trims, so splitting them risks two passes fighting over the same paragraph.
  **(A) THE CAPACITY REPLY IS AN AUXILIARY BILLED AS A PREDICTION.** "What the Tool Demands of Its User" closes: "Conscious animals below the capacity threshold should lack deep recursion, and they do." The dissociation objection (conscious animals lack recursion) is the sharpest available evidence against Synthesis pillar two, *directional dependence*. The reply adds a free parameter — consciousness necessary but not sufficient, expanded working memory the second ingredient — and then presents its fit as a confirmed prediction. It cannot be one: every non-human in the sample is below threshold and lacks recursion, the one above-threshold case is the species that has recursion, so **no observation in the available sample could disconfirm it**, and a purely non-conscious syntactic-architecture account predicts the identical distribution. That would be an ordinary cost honestly borne — except the Synthesis eight paragraphs later convicts rivals of needing "epicycles" while the article's own reply to its sharpest objection is one. **Fix: mark the reply as an auxiliary (the pattern is *consistent with* the capacity account and does not discriminate it from a non-conscious architectural account), and soften or qualify the "without epicycles" charge so one standard is applied.** ⚠️ The 2026-09-02 review already got the *citation* hedges installed here ("a contested cross-species comparison" is present and correct — do not touch it); this is the structural cost, which no pass has raised.
  **OPTIONAL BUT RECOMMENDED, AND IT DISCHARGES A SECOND STANDING FINDING:** the article names no condition under which the *tool* picture fails — a point raised by the Popper persona on 2026-09-02 and still live (greps: `unconscious manipulation` = 0, `would collapse` = 0). The falsifier is sitting in the article's own reference 1: a demonstration of unconscious WM ***manipulation*** (not maintenance) would take out the directional-dependence face. Naming it costs one clause and earns the prediction language back.
  **(B) "FIVE STRUCTURALLY DIFFERENT MATERIALS" — THE ARTICLE NAMES THREE.** In "Five Ways of Failing" the gloss enumerates the materials as "content with dimensions language lacks resolution for, content bound to non-discursive states, content with no linguistic correlate at all" — three — and the next paragraph asserts "A tool that fails in five structurally different ways against five structurally different materials is telling you about the materials." The one-to-one mapping the argument needs leaves *mismatch register* and *degradation through repetition* unassigned. Compounding it: the five-mode taxonomy has **no source anywhere in the corpus** — `topics/phenomenology-of-linguistic-failure`'s references are Gendlin, Wittgenstein (*Tractatus*), Nagel, Levine, Chalmers, none of which supply a taxonomy — so it is the Map's own phenomenological carving. That is fine as phenomenology and not fine as a *count* doing evidential work, since a different carving yields three or seven. **Fix: either extend the gloss so each mode maps to its own material, or drop the numeric framing where it is evidential — the argument survives on qualitative heterogeneity without the integer. Carry the lead's existing hedge ("at least five") into Synthesis pillar three, where it is currently dropped, and mark the taxonomy as the Map's own carving rather than a finding.**
  ⚠️ **Note the asymmetry worth preserving**: the article discounts the convergence of the five *faces* via [the common-cause null](/project/common-cause-null/) with real care. That is the article's best move — **do not weaken it.** The defect is only that no comparable discount is applied to the granularity of the five *modes*.

### ✓ 2026-09-20: `apex/cross-modal-capability-division` L96/L100 carry three publisher-verified citation defects propagated from the vestibular spoke — the spoke is fixed, the apex is not
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/apex/cross-modal-capability-division.md
- **Notes**: Three defects were web-verified at the publisher of record during the 2026-09-20 deep review of `topics/vestibular-consciousness-and-the-interface` and fixed **in the spoke only** — the apex reuses the same seven cites and repeats all three verbatim-in-substance. Corpus sweep (`grep -F`, printed counts, `obsidian/` + `archive/`, reviews/workflow excluded as echo): the defects live in exactly **two** live files, the spoke (now fixed) and this apex. No third locus.
  **(1) L96 — `caloric` over-attribution (CRITICAL).** Text: "galvanic and caloric stimulation modulate illusory hand ownership (Lopez, Lenggenhager & Blanke, 2010)". **Verified**: Lopez, Lenggenhager & Blanke 2010 (Conscious. Cogn. 19(1):33–47, PMID 20047844) tested **galvanic vestibular stimulation only** — "we investigated whether galvanic vestibular stimulation (GVS) interferes with the mechanisms underlying ownership, touch, and the localization of one's own hand … by using the 'rubber hand illusion' paradigm", and the result is polarity-specific: "**only left anodal GVS**" increased illusory ownership. Caloric stimulation appears in that paper only as reviewed *prior* work on patients, not as its own RHI result. Fix, **−1 word**: drop "and caloric" → "galvanic stimulation modulates illusory hand ownership".
  **(2) L100 — Laurens & Droulez prior mislocated (CRITICAL).** Text: "Laurens and Droulez model vestibular perception as Bayesian estimation **against a gravity prior**". **Verified**: the L&D 2007 abstract states the model is "related to the statistics of natural head movements … creat[ing] a perceptual bias in favour of **low velocity and acceleration**". An OA re-implementation of the identical model (Irmak et al. 2023, PMC10258185, §"Particle filter model", written from L&D's own notation) states that "the magnitude of gravity |G| … is **assumed to be constant** at −9.81 ms⁻²" while "the particles that are closest to **zero acceleration and angular velocity** are weighted the highest". Gravity is a fixed constant in the internal model, **not** the prior. The full text is paywalled (Biol. Cybern.; unpaywall `is_oa: false`), so this rests on the publisher abstract plus the OA re-implementation. Fix, **+1 word**: "Bayesian estimation under low-motion priors, with gravity as the default reading of an ambiguous otolith signal" — or any word-neutral variant that stops calling the gravity term a prior.
  **(3) L96 — Cento et al. 2026 strength (CRITICAL).** Text: "vestibular disturbance **triggers** depersonalization (Cento et al., 2026)". **Verified** against the publisher abstract (J. Vestib. Res. 36(5):326–351, DOI 10.1177/09574271251412707, PMID 41499789): it is a PRISMA systematic review of 23 studies whose own conclusion is "vestibular alterations **may contribute to** the emergence of DD symptoms", explicitly hedged by "substantial heterogeneity in the studied populations, experimental paradigms, and assessment methodologies limits clear conclusions". "Triggers" upgrades a guarded association to a causal claim the source declines. Fix, **+0 words**: "triggers" → "accompanies", or "+2": "may contribute to".
  **(4) Metadata completion, +2 words, optional**: the apex's Cento References entry (L170) also lacks volume/issue/pages. The verified form is *Journal of Vestibular Research*, **36(5), 326–351**. Do **not** renumber the apex's numbered reference list — correct in place.
  **What was already verified real-correct and needs no action**: de Vignemont 2024 (all quotes verbatim at the live SEP entry, revised Fri Sep 20 2024), Pfeiffer/Serino/Blanke 2014 (all quotes verbatim at PMC4028995), Blanke & Arzy 2005, Blanke 2012, Cento et al. 2026 metadata, Laurens & Droulez 2007 metadata. Full per-cite ledger in the review file named above — reuse it rather than re-verifying.

### ✓ 2026-09-20: The Appetitive Void cites Kunda and Ellis as support when both cut against it, and ducks the 2020s debiasing evidence entirely
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/voids/appetitive-void.md
- **Notes**: **THIS IS THE ONE FINDING IN THE CLAUDE LEG NOT ALREADY COVERED BY THE FOUR CHATGPT TASKS IN THIS BATCH** — checked against all four before minting. It is also the most securely verified finding of the whole cycle: every empirical claim under it was confirmed at publisher of record, not aggregators.
  **"LISTED, NOT ENGAGED" IS DRIVER-VERIFIED AND MECHANICAL.** `Kunda` occurs exactly once in `obsidian/voids/appetitive-void.md` and `Ellis` exactly once (`grep -icF`), and **both occurrences are the reference-list lines themselves** — L145 (`Kunda, Z. (1990). "The Case for Motivated Reasoning." *Psychological Bulletin*, 108(3), 480-498.`) and L144 (`Ellis, J. (2022). "Motivated reasoning and the ethics of belief." *Philosophy Compass*, 17(6), e12828.`). Neither is named, quoted or argued with anywhere in the body. The article carries them as if they were support.
  **BOTH ACTUALLY CUT AGAINST THE THESIS.** ✓ Verified at source: Kunda's published abstract states that people "are more likely to arrive at conclusions that they want to arrive at, but their ability to do so is **constrained** by their ability to construct seemingly reasonable justifications for these conclusions" (DOI `10.1037/0033-2909.108.3.480`, confirmed at Crossref). Kunda 1990 is a **constraint** thesis — motivated reasoning is bounded by what the evidence will bear — which is close to the opposite of the unbounded constitutive claim the article advances. Ellis 2022 (DOI `10.1111/phc3.12828`, Jon Ellis, confirmed) argues motivated reasoning is not always epistemically problematic. The article needs to answer both, and currently does not mention either.
  **THE STRONGEST DUCKED OBJECTION, WITH THE NUMBERS VERIFIED VERBATIM.** Rathje, S., Roozenbeek, J., Van Bavel, J. J., & van der Linden, S. (2023), "Accuracy and social motivations shape judgements of (mis)information", *Nature Human Behaviour* **7**(6), 892–903, DOI `10.1038/s41562-023-01540-w`. ✓ Crossref confirms journal, volume, issue, pages, all four authors **and author order**; the quoted findings match the publisher abstract **verbatim** (checked via OpenAlex's abstract index, not an aggregator): across four experiments (*n* = 3,364) "Financial incentives improved accuracy and reduced partisan bias in judgements of headlines by about 30%, primarily by increasing the perceived accuracy of true news from the opposing party (d = 0.47)"; incentives "closed the gap in accuracy between conservatives and liberals by 52%"; and "A non-financial accuracy motivation intervention was also effective, suggesting that motivation-based interventions are scalable." `Rathje` = 0 in the article. **A trivial incentive measurably corrects the exact distortion the article calls uncorrectable** — and it is the empirical vindication of the Kunda constraint thesis the article already cites.
  **DIRECT-REFUTATION REMIT.** This is an opponent-engagement weakness of the clearest kind: the strongest live objection is not merely unanswered but is sitting in the article's own bibliography. Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/). Identify what kind of engagement the issue calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared.
  ⚠️ **THE OBVIOUS REPLY IS A TRAP AND THE SIBLING TASK OWNS IT.** The available move — "the desire for accuracy is itself a desire" — converts the thesis into the trivial, unfalsifiable reading and contradicts the article's own "What Would Challenge This View?" section. That dilemma is scoped in the **P2 falsifiers task** addendum in this same batch; do not re-argue it here, cite it. The honest resolution is likely the modal downgrade the **P1 claim-ladder task** installs, so these three tasks should be sequenced together and the concession written once.
  **PHILOSOPHY-OF-DESIRE COUNTERWEIGHT — lower priority, add only if headroom survives.** `Schroeder` = 0 and `Frankfurt` = 0 in the article. Schroeder's reward-based theory (*Three Faces of Desire*, OUP 2004) treats desire as a discrete state of the reward system rather than the frame of all representation; Frankfurt's second-order volitions ("Freedom of the Will and the Concept of a Person", *Journal of Philosophy* **68**(1), 1971) supply the reflective distance the void denies. ⚠️ Neither was verified at publisher this run — verify before citing. Note this strand partly overlaps the physicalist-fractionation work already scoped in the claim-ladder task; prefer a single sentence with two citations over a new section.
  **LENGTH — READ THIS BEFORE WRITING.** 2270 words against voids soft 2000 / hard 3000 / critical 4000; `length.py` fires on `>= hard`, so the usable ceiling is 2999 and there are **729 words of headroom shared across what is now FIVE Appetitive Void tasks**, not four. This task is the only one in the batch that is **purely additive**, so it is the one the budget binds hardest. Two mitigations: the P2 falsifiers task is mostly subtraction and should be executed **first** to bank headroom, and the Kunda/Ellis repair can be done largely by *rewriting the existing reference-list framing and adding one engaging paragraph* rather than a new section. Budget ~180 words for the Rathje/Kunda engagement and treat everything else here as optional.

### ✓ 2026-09-20: The Appetitive Void's falsifiers are non-diagnostic and its convergence language predates [P-V3](/positions/voids-as-evidence/#p-v3)'s suspension
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/voids/appetitive-void.md
- **Notes**: **THE SELF-SEALING CHARGE (§4.1–4.3) IS THE PART TO TAKE SERIOUSLY.** The void is said to be invisible because it structures every attempt to observe it — which may be a genuine transcendental point, but it also means failure to find a desire-free condition confirms the thesis while any candidate desire-free condition gets redescribed as the product of a desire to find one. Each of the article's three gestured falsifiers is currently insulated: "desire-free" cannot be shown if every experimental instruction or reporting act counts as appetite; an artificial system always has a training objective if optimisation history counts as desire; patterned motivated reasoning shows influence, not constitution. The repair the reviewer asks for is specific and is the right one: say **which auxiliary explanations the article forbids itself** after a negative result.
  **"THESE EXCEPTIONS PROVE THE RULE" IS VERBATIM AT L56** (driver-verified), applied to Schopenhauer's aesthetic contemplation, compassion and ascetic resignation. Under strict constitutivity a genuine exception is a counterexample, not a confirmation. Either weaken the thesis to "ordinary cognition is normally will-serving" — which the reviewer notes is far more defensible — or drop the move. Same asymmetry at §3.4 on contemplative states: that a prior desire motivated entry, or a later desire seeks recovery, says nothing about whether appetite was present *during* the state. A desire to fall asleep does not put that desire inside sleep phenomenology.
  **CONVERGENCE — READ THE VERIFICATION NOTES FIRST; THE REVIEWER PARTLY MISREAD THIS ONE.** ⚠️ ChatGPT's §5 charges the article with manufacturing evidential independence. Driver-verified, it does not: L122 says the traditions "are not independent instruments, either" and that their agreement "carries the evidential weight of one well-replicated introspective report **rather than six independent confirmations** of a mind-independent fact". The phrase the reviewer wants replaced is being used as a *ceiling*, not a floor (prior-review-file-carries-stability-notes-that-preempt-your-suspicion). **Do not rewrite the convergence framing wholesale.** Two narrower things are genuinely owed: (a) the same sentence's claim that replication "rules out the idiosyncratic and the culture-bound" is too strong — intellectual transmission, broad translation choices and selection of congenial traditions remain live explanations, so soften "rules out"; (b) `P-V1` = 0, `P-V3` = 0, `voids-as-evidence` = 0 in this article (driver-verified), so cite the register. Note the timing honestly: **[P-V3](/positions/voids-as-evidence/#p-v3)'s cumulative-weight step was suspended on 2026-09-15, four days after this article's last revision**, so the article predates the suspension rather than defying it — frame the edit as bringing it into conformity, not as correcting a violation. Bare position ids autolink, so `P-V1` needs no wikilink (bare-position-ids-autolink-themselves-wikilinks-are-redundant).
  **TENET HYGIENE (§4.4, §4.5).** Two phrases to fix. First, "appetite as a feature of incarnation" (L108) is presented as though dualism predicts desire-free awareness; it does not discriminate — a physicalist can posit a neural condition with awareness and little motivation, and a dualist can hold every nonphysical mind inherently appetitive. Mark it as the speculation it is. Second, "bidirectional interaction turned inward" imports Tenet 3, which concerns causal relations between consciousness and physical processes, to describe motivational modulation *within* cognition. Either supply the mechanism connecting the two or drop the phrase. ⚠️ Check `obsidian/positions/` before conceding anything here — outer reviewers on this corpus recurrently attack mechanisms the Map already disclaims (gemini-outer-review-attacks-mechanisms-the-map-disclaims).

  **ADDENDUM 2026-09-20 — THE SELF-SEALING CHARGE HAS A SHARPER TWIN (`reviews/outer-review-2026-09-20-claude-opus-5.md`).** Claude Opus 5 converges on self-sealing but presses a **distinct** charge the §4.1–4.3 material above does not reach, and it is the one item Claude itself says would remain open even if everything else were fixed. Two parts:
  (a) **THE TRIVIALITY DILEMMA, stated against the article's own falsifiability section.** The article's pre-emption — "even the desire for objectivity is itself a desire" (L68 carries the "Even the desire for truth is a desire" form) — converts the thesis to "some motivation always co-occurs with cognition", which is unfalsifiable, **directly contradicting the article's own "What Would Challenge This View?" section**. And co-occurrence is not constitution. So the article must choose: keep the pre-emption and drop the falsifiability claim, or keep falsifiability and stop deploying the pre-emption as a universal solvent. This is the same self-sealing structure as §4.3 but aimed at a specific sentence, which makes it actionable.
  (b) **SELF-REFUTATION, which the article currently aestheticises.** The article raises the regress (the thought "your thinking is desire-shaped" is itself desire-shaped) and treats it as confirmatory, closing with "The deepest question remains open". Claude's objection: if the void thesis is itself desire-constituted and so cannot track mind-independent truth about cognition, we have no more reason to accept it than any other appetitively-shaped output. The only exit is a Hales-style object-language/meta-language split holding the structural claim true across all standpoints — **but that concedes at least one non-desire-relative structural truth, which contradicts the universal constitutive claim**. Either horn is survivable; converting it to atmosphere is not. ⚠️ Note this is an argument the article should **answer**, not necessarily concede — and the honest answer may be that it weakens the thesis to the (b)/(c) modal rungs the sibling claim-ladder task installs, in which case the two tasks should be executed together and the self-refutation paragraph written once. Keep it to ~80 words; the shared headroom is thin.
  **ADDENDUM 2026-09-20 — THIRD REVIEWER SUPPLIES THE CONCRETE FALSIFIER PROBE THIS TASK WAS MISSING (`reviews/outer-review-2026-09-20-gemini-2-5-pro.md`).** This is Gemini 2.5 Pro's highest-value contribution and it lands squarely inside this task's remit. The article's **first falsifier** (L116) names "neuroscientific studies of experienced meditators" showing "cognition with no appetitive structure" as the decisive test — and then cites nothing that has attempted it. Driver-verified: `nirodha` = 0 and `samapatti` = 0 in the article. **The literature exists and is verified at publisher of record:** Laukkonen, R. E., Sacchet, M. D., Barendregt, H., Devaney, K. J., Chowdhury, A., & Slagter, H. A. (2023), "Cessations of consciousness in meditation: Advancing a scientific understanding of nirodha samāpatti," *Progress in Brain Research*, pp. 61–87, DOI `10.1016/bs.pbr.2022.12.007` (title and page range confirmed at Crossref). Gemini's rhetorical point is sharp and fair: the article "weaponizes Buddhist theory (*tanha*) while ignoring the Buddhist empirical endpoint (*nirodha*)".
  ⚠️ **BUT GEMINI'S INFERENCE OVERREACHES — ADOPT THE CITATION, NOT THE VERDICT.** It claims cessation "demonstrates definitively" that desire is an activity rather than constitutive fabric. That does not follow as stated: *nirodha samāpatti* is reported as a cessation **of experience altogether**, so it is not an instance of desire-free *cognition* — it is arguably the absence of cognition, which the constitutive thesis can absorb without strain. The honest use is the one this task already wants: a falsifier is non-diagnostic when the framework can absorb both outcomes, and cessation is the sharpest available illustration — it is the best candidate anyone has produced, **and the article can still absorb it**, which is exactly the self-sealing problem. Naming the study and saying what it would and would not settle is worth more than either ignoring it or conceding to it, and it converts falsifier 1 from a gesture into something with a literature attached. Budget ~60–80 words; this task is otherwise subtraction-first and should stay roughly length-neutral.

### ✓ 2026-09-20: The Appetitive Void equivocates across ten senses of "desire" — install a claim ladder and propagate the Wanting/Liking constructs
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/voids/appetitive-void.md
- **Notes**: **THIS IS THE REPORT'S CENTRAL CHARGE AND THE REASON IT RECOMMENDS MAJOR REVISION.** The article runs four distinct claims together — motivation sometimes influences cognition; motivational orientation pervades ordinary cognition; some appetitive structure is necessary for any cognition; desire may be inseparable from consciousness — and draws its support from the first two while rhetorically advancing the last two. The decisive gap is **causal influence vs constitution**: that fear affects height estimates or that goals alter information search shows motivation can help determine processing, not that every percept or state of awareness is partly made of wanting. The reviewer's fix is a claim ladder distinguishing phenomenological, representational, functional, causal-modulation and metaphysical constitution, with the article stating which rung it defends. Note the dilemma that makes this urgent: if "desire" means any goal, prior preference or optimisation criterion, the thesis covers thermostats and says nothing distinctive about consciousness; if it means phenomenal wanting, the evidence does not establish ubiquity.
  **THE CONSTRUCT-PROPAGATION FAILURE IS DRIVER-VERIFIED AND IS THE STRONGEST FINDING IN THE REPORT.** In `obsidian/voids/appetitive-void.md`: `wanting-liking` = 0, `Wanting and Liking` = 0, `Berridge` = 0, `incentive salience` = 0 (grep -icF, against a positive control of `desire` = 20, `wanting` = 5, so these are real absences). Meanwhile `obsidian/topics/wanting-liking-and-the-value-in-mechanism-fork.md` carries `last_deep_review: 2026-08-27` and `modified: 2026-09-05` — it was modernised **before** this article's 2026-09-11 revision, and the revision did not pick it up. That article already distinguishes wanting, liking, prediction and learning and already states a physicalist mechanism-only rival. **This is not a missing hyperlink.** Its distinctions cut directly against the article's unitary "appetite", because wanting dissociates from liking, from expected value and from conscious report. Link it at or near the first definition of appetite and let its vocabulary govern the article.
  **DIRECT-REFUTATION REMIT.** The physicalist fractionation objection (§3.1) is an opponent-engagement weakness: contemporary motivational neuroscience does not show one process answering to the cross-traditional "appetite" but separates incentive salience, hedonic impact, reinforcement learning, reward prediction, effort allocation, homeostatic need and conscious desire. Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/). Identify what kind of engagement the issue calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. The honest answer here is likely that the physicalist alternative is not merely "the same data with a different ontology" — it supplies a *finer-grained decomposition* of what the article groups together, which is a real cost the article should own rather than absorb.
  **ACTIVE INFERENCE (§2.6) — trim one overclaim.** The article's "collapses the boundary between prediction and preference" is stronger than the formalism licenses: expected free energy puts epistemic and pragmatic value in a common objective while keeping beliefs about states, preferences over outcomes, policy posteriors, information gain and pragmatic value formally distinct. And a technical "prior preference" is not a felt desire — a nonconscious controller can embody outcome preferences without experiencing wanting. Rewrite to the accurate, still-interesting claim.
  **LENGTH:** 2270 words measured against voids 2000/3000/4000 (`soft_warning`); usable ceiling 2999, **729 words of headroom shared across the four Appetitive Void tasks in this batch**. A full terminology table (improvement 2) plus a claim ladder plus a physicalist-rival section will not all fit. Prefer the claim ladder (highest argumentative yield) and a compact inline gloss over a full table, and consider that the Wanting/Liking link lets the article *delegate* rather than restate the taxonomy — a piped wikilink installs a reciprocal at zero word cost (piped-wikilink-installs-a-reciprocal-at-zero-word-cost).
  **ADDENDUM 2026-09-20 — SECOND REVIEWER CONVERGES ON THE CENTRAL CHARGE (`reviews/outer-review-2026-09-20-claude-opus-5.md`).** Claude Opus 5 independently makes equivocation its headline finding, which makes this the **strongest cross-reviewer convergence of the cycle** and confirms this task as the batch's priority. It cuts the ladder differently and more cheaply than the five-rung version above: **three theses, not ten senses** — (a) desire makes stepping outside impossible *in principle*; (b) one *never fully succeeds in practice*; (c) one *cannot verify* that one has succeeded. Its verdict is that the article's evidence supports (b) and (c) only, while its title, description and framing assert (a). Two driver-verified anchors: `impossibility of stepping outside` = 1 in the article, and the frontmatter `description` itself asserts the strong reading ("the constitutive framework from which thought cannot escape") — so **the overclaim lives on a navigation surface too, and the description must be fixed alongside the body** (navigation-surfaces-carry-unreviewed-claims). Claude also notes the article's own conceded partial exits (Spinoza's adequate ideas, Nietzsche's "local contours visible", meditative "glimpses at the edge") are each inconsistent with (a) — so the repair is largely **subtraction and requalification**, which is friendly to the shared word budget. If the executing pass can only fit one ladder, prefer this three-rung modal one: it is cheaper, it is what both reviewers independently demanded, and the five-rung version can be delegated to the Wanting/Liking link.
  **ACTIVE INFERENCE — A NAMED SOURCE FOR THE §2.6 OVERCLAIM, AND IT CUTS DEEPER.** The trim scoped above now has a citation behind it. Claude argues predictive processing, which the article recruits as its strongest ally, arguably **defeats** the strong reading: desire-priors are precisely the precision-weighted, revisable quantities the system can down-weight in favour of sensory evidence, so a framework whose defining operation is reweighting is built *for* correction, not against it. Source: Clark, A. (2020), "Beyond Desire? Agency, Choice, and the Predictive Mind", *Australasian Journal of Philosophy* **98**(1), 1–15, DOI `10.1080/00048402.2019.1602661`. ✓ **Driver-verified at Crossref, and both quoted fragments match the publisher abstract verbatim**: desires "emerge as webs of prior 'beliefs' that sculpt probabilistic predictions" and "Importantly, a single construct here plays the role of belief and desire." Note the double edge precisely — Clark supports the article's claim that the prediction/preference **format** collapses, while undercutting the claim that bias is **inescapable**. `Clark` = 0 in the article; add the reference.

Two specific corrections the reviewer supplies: (a) in the dependent-origination formula craving (*taṇhā*) arises **conditioned by feeling**, with consciousness, name-and-form, the six sense bases and contact preceding it — so early Buddhism cannot be read as teaching that craving constitutes perception; (b) the article's "foundational paradox" of desiring an end to desire conflates *taṇhā* (compulsive craving) with *chanda* (aspiration/intention), and SN 51.15 raises and answers the apparent regress directly via an instrumental analogy rather than treating it as fatal. Nietzsche (§2.4): the *Genealogy* III perspectivism quotation is authentic, but "will to power drives all knowledge" needs a precise passage given the textual problems around posthumously assembled material — and the article omits Nietzsche's constructive move, that admitting **more** affects and perspectives increases objectivity. But the article slides conatus → appetite → conscious desire; Spinoza's own scholium makes desire the *conscious form* of appetite. So "remove desire and being disappears" and "the substance of conscious existence" both overreach (and individual minds are modes, not substances). Also correct the "partial exit" gloss: adequate understanding reorganises and activates striving, it does not suspend conatus.

On that arithmetic the direction demand does not fit inside the ceiling bounding outcome selection…"*
  **WHY IT IS WRONG.** The product is licensed only if the crossover **drifts** between placements — which requires the neural bath correlation time that the concept page itself concedes nobody has measured. **A regular observation train specifies its interval once, not per placement**, so the multiplication inflates the price by roughly thirteen orders of magnitude.
  ✅ **THE CONCLUSION SURVIVES WITHOUT IT — do not retract the argument.** The concept page's repair keeps the verdict on the un-multiplied arithmetic: **log₂(0.3 / 2.5×10⁻¹⁴) ≈ 43 bits** to locate a single crossover, against **10 bits/s × 0.3 s = 3 bits** available — still an order-of-magnitude shortfall. **Port the hedge, not a retraction**: the concept page now reads *"That product is an upper bound rather than a settled price: an agent emitting a regular train would specify the interval once rather than each placement"*. ⚠️ **Match that wording rather than inventing new phrasing**, so the two pages agree (i-widen-retractions-and-upgrade-coiners-into-proponents).

### ✓ 2026-09-19: `topics/memory-channel-interface-evidence` — the recovery-asymmetry comparative ledger is inflated on both sides, and the Tenet 3 argument does not discriminate
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/memory-channel-interface-evidence.md
- **Notes**: See [pessimistic-2026-09-19-memory-channel-interface-evidence](/reviews/pessimistic-2026-09-19-memory-channel-interface-evidence/) P2 and P4. All three edits are length-neutral or length-reducing.
  **(a) CREDIT SIDE — the filter reading "derives" nothing.** Locus verbatim: `may depend on the direction and cause of change`. That clause permits *every* ordering, including the mirror-symmetric one the production account is faulted for — yet the surrounding prose awards comparative credit (`appears to derive a direction-sensitive signature from its core architecture without per-case additions`, `*direct* where production's is *additive*`). ℹ️ **The article already makes the matching concession on the substrate-state face** — `Bandwidth and reconstruction cost are ranked partly by the very ordering they are then invoked to explain` — and simply does not carry it to the direction face, where it is *worse* (on the substrate face a ranking at least exists; on the direction face nothing is assigned). **Fix = state what the bandwidth/reconstruction-cost architecture *forbids*, or downgrade "derive" → "is consistent with" and carry the §122 concession forward in full rather than gesturing at it with `subject to the same unmeasured-assignment caveat`.**
  **(b) DEBIT SIDE — the additivity charge is roughly double.** Locus verbatim: `hysteresis terms for the bistable switch, a dying-process neuromodulatory surge for terminal lucidity, a stochastic reconstitution model for emergence, a connectivity-gating story for dissociation`. **Hysteresis is a generic property of any bistable nonlinear system** — and the article's own source **Sepúlveda et al. 2019** presents neural inertia as a property of circuits, established independently of the mind-brain question. **Stochastic emergence timing (Stone et al. 2025) is the expected behaviour of a noisy bistable system crossing a threshold**, i.e. a consequence of that same commitment, not a second auxiliary. Genuine additions = **two** (terminal-lucidity surge; dissociation connectivity gating), not four. The phrase `the four direction-of-interface signatures` in §144 repeats the inflated count. **Also straw-target**: `predicts, in its simplest form, that recovery should retrace degradation` attributes a position no production theorist holds. **Fix = recount to two, name bistable-with-noise dynamics as one pre-existing commitment covering both anaesthesia signatures, and replace the "simplest form" target with a position actually defended.**
  **(c) TENET 3 NON-DISCRIMINATION.** Locus verbatim: `the most vulnerable channel would be the input-richest one` (§ *Relation to Site Perspective* → Bidirectional Interaction). Two problems: it is **asserted with no argument** (why should passive reception make the input-richest channel most fragile? the opposite intuition — vulnerability where *transmission* is thinnest — is at least as available); and **it may not be a contrast at all**, since autonoetic re-experiencing plausibly *is* the input-richest channel, in which case reception-only and bidirectional predict the *same* channel and the observation supports neither.
  ✅ **The paragraph's closing conditional is GOOD and must be preserved** — `Should the autonoetic channel prove to return first under falling substrate function and last under rising…` is genuinely discriminating and correctly marked as undischarged. **Cheapest fix = delete the unargued counterfactual clause and rest the Tenet 3 engagement on that conditional alone.**
  ℹ️ **NOT a Tenet-5 finding — do not add a parsimony guard here.** I checked: `Occam`/`occams-limits`/`simplicity`/`Tenet 5` all 0 in this article (control: 238 topic files cite `occams-limits`), and the 2026-09-19 tenet sweep did not cover it. **But the locus is already triple-guarded** (`a parsimony point at the mechanism-cost layer, not positive evidence the filter reading is true, because a production account willing to pay the per-case cost absorbs every signature`) plus an outright disclaimer (`The dualist reading is not more parsimonious — interface architecture is not simple`). Judged **in contract**; flagging it would be the lexical-vs-structural false high. The governing doctrine question is also blocked on the `NEEDS-HUMAN (doctrine) 2026-09-19` item.

### ✓ 2026-09-19: `topics/memory-channel-interface-evidence` counts five independent confirmations where its own anchor citation supplies a shared variable — and its table contains a counterexample to the claim stated nine lines below it
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/memory-channel-interface-evidence.md
- **Notes**: Two findings in the same region, both length-neutral or length-reducing. See [pessimistic-2026-09-19-memory-channel-interface-evidence](/reviews/pessimistic-2026-09-19-memory-channel-interface-evidence/) P1 and P3 for full reasoning.
  **(a) CONVERGENCE DOUBLE-COUNTING.** Locus verbatim: `share no common neural cause, yet they converge on the same ordering of channel vulnerability` (§ *The Structural Argument*). The article's own anchor citation **Sarasso et al. 2015** measures *cortical-response complexity* — a single variable tracking presence/absence of experience across propofol, xenon and ketamine — and the same integration/complexity measure is the standard bridge between **anaesthesia and NREM sleep**, two of the five convergence members. So the five are not five independent instances. This matters because §144 settles the whole comparison on the cumulative count (`The cumulative cost across the hierarchy, the dissociation architecture, terminal lucidity, the propofol/ketamine divergence, and the four direction-of-interface signatures is where the comparison comes due`).
  ✅ **WHAT SURVIVES — do not over-correct.** The **dissociative rows do not reduce**; there is no substrate-degradation variable to appeal to, and the article already calls them `the hierarchy's hardest test for production theories`. **Fix = narrow `share no common neural cause` to a claim about *molecular* mechanism, concede the shared cortical-integration variable for anaesthesia/NREM in one clause, and relocate the argumentative weight onto the dissociative rows.** This makes the article more honest without weakening its best case.
  **(b) INTERNAL CONTRADICTION IN THE TABLE.** Row verbatim: `| Ketamine "disconnected" state | Preserved | Variable | Preserved but content-decoupled |` — noetic **Variable**, autonoetic **Preserved**, i.e. the ordering *inverts* on that row. Claim verbatim nine lines below: `autonoetic goes first under degradation`. The existing hedge `but in none of them arbitrarily` does not cover an inversion. Note the article **does** handle the milder depersonalisation case (by decomposing autonoetic into content + mine-ness + pastness) but gives ketamine no equivalent treatment despite leaning on it as `the sharpest case within it`.
  **Fix = preferred option (a): qualify the ordering claim to the substrate-degradation cases and exempt the *decoupling* cases (ketamine, depersonalisation, DID) as a distinct signature type (~15 words).** Ketamine reads naturally as a *coupling* failure rather than a *channel* failure — that is stronger material than an unremarked contradiction.
  ⚠️ **LENGTH: DO NOT CONDENSE AND DO NOT MINT A CONDENSE TASK.** `analyze_length` reports 4449w vs hard 4000, but this is a **settled false warning** — re-measured this run: **prose 3887w, apparatus 571w**; prose is 113w *under* the threshold. Recorded as a stability note by the 2026-08-08 deep review and carried by 06-16 / 07-12 / 07-20.

### ✓ 2026-09-19: `concepts/haecceity` and `positions/individuation-and-subjecthood` contradict each other on whether a process-only subject can carry the No-Many-Worlds objection
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/haecceity.md
- **Notes**: **A STRAIGHT CONTRADICTION BETWEEN AN ARTICLE AND THE POSITIONS REGISTER. Both sides verified verbatim this run.**
  **(a) `concepts/haecceity`** says a process-only subject suffices: *"the particularity grounding identity **need not require a permanent soul**… Being this conscious subject is a primitive fact—your haecceity—**whether that fact attaches to an enduring substance or an impermanent experiential process**."* (`process haecceit` appears 15 times in the file, so this is the article's central move, not an aside.)
  **(b) `positions/individuation-and-subjecthood`** says the opposite, twice: a *"deflationary, fragmented, or **process-only** account of the subject… **would simultaneously weaken the No-Many-Worlds**"* objection; and *"retiring [P-I1](/positions/individuation-and-subjecthood/#p-i1) for a deflationary, fragmented or **process-only** subject **leaves the objection with nothing to discriminate among**."*
  **They cannot both stand.** Either process haecceitism does ground the indexical objection — in which case [P-I1](/positions/individuation-and-subjecthood/#p-i1)/[P-I2](/positions/individuation-and-subjecthood/#p-i2)'s *Would shift if* clauses are wrong — or it does not, in which case `haecceity`'s central refinement overclaims. ⚠️ **This is not a wording mismatch**: `tenets/background-commitments` Posit One and `positions/subject-census` [P-SC3](/positions/subject-census/#p-sc3) both route the Tenet 4 objection through *diachronic subject persistence* specifically, which is exactly what an "impermanent experiential process" declines to supply.
  🛑 **DO NOT RESOLVE IT BY EDITING THE REGISTER.** Adjudicate on the argument first, and note the register is the harder surface to change: it mandates a dated prose `Updated` note per amendment (positions-register-cannot-take-edits-length-neutrally) and **`positions/individuation-and-subjecthood` is already 3938 words against a 4000 positions hard ceiling — 1439 words OVER the usable ceiling of 2499** (positions thresholds are an explicit 1500/2500/4000 entry, not inherited defaults). Any register edit is length-blocked before it starts.

DOI `10.1007/s11098-022-01804-7`** — targets the article's appeal to the self-evidence of phenomenal states; Kammerer argues the Moorean move begs the question against illusionism because the very obviousness it invokes is what a functional theory of introspection predicts.

The constitutive-physicalist reading holds that phenomenal concepts can be constituted by the brain states they pick out, which would neutralise the knowledge-argument step without any non-physical fact. **This is the sharpest of the three**: it names an assumption the article makes rather than a source it omits.
  ⚠️ Take only these three from the Gemini review. Its other four cited sources are defective — see that review's Verification Notes: Schlosshauer is mis-dated (2019, not 2022) and mis-paginated, the structuralism paper is attributed to Lyre but is by Niccolò Negro and argues *against* the position it is cited to support, and the Gładziejewski citation is attached to a pain-asymbolia claim it cannot support.
  **Added by /combine-outer-reviews from the Claude Opus 5 leg — ALL FOUR ARE UNVERIFIED.** That leg's own Verification Notes state plainly that none of these was fetched this pass ("Chalmers 2020 … Saad 2019, Kammerer 2019/2021, Díaz 2021, Clark/Friston/Wilkinson 2019 and Laukkonen/Friston/Chandaria 2025 were **not** fetched this pass"), and it explicitly routes verification to *this* task rather than duplicating it. **Resolve each at the publisher of record before citing; do not carry the reviewer's metadata into the article unchecked** (ai_citation_metadata_unreliable).
  (d) **Díaz, R. (2021). "Do People Think Consciousness Poses a Hard Problem? Empirical Evidence on the Meta-Problem of Consciousness." *Journal of Consciousness Studies* 28(3-4), 55-75.** Claude quotes its conclusion as *"The results of four studies will show that (1) problem intuitions are not widespread, and (2) when they arise, they do so because of factors that are unrelated to the nature of consciousness."* **This is the second reviewer to reach the article's challenge-condition #3 independently** — ChatGPT got there via Berent 2024 (already listed above, Crossref-verified). Two reviewers, two different empirical literatures, one conclusion: the article's "if societies with different conceptual frameworks lacked problem intuitions entirely" falsifier is no longer hypothetical and should be rewritten as live mixed evidence rather than a future test. Claude also names a 2026 cross-cultural follow-up (Kuś et al., *Philosophical Psychology*, DOI `10.1080/09515089.2026.2710862`) which it reports is itself cautious — *"We think it is too early to tell"* — so if it is cited at all, cite it at that strength.
  (e) **Clark, A., Friston, K. & Wilkinson, S. (2019). "Bayesing Qualia: Consciousness as Inference, Not Raw Datum." *JCS* 26(9-10), 19-33** and **Laukkonen, R., Friston, K. & Chandaria, S. (2025). "A beautiful loop: An active inference theory of consciousness." *Neuroscience & Biobehavioral Reviews* 176, 106296.** Claude treats these as a *rival topic-neutral meta-problem solution* the article never engages, quoting the 2019 abstract: *"We present and defend a solution to the meta-problem."* ⚠️ **Scope-split this one** (outer-reviewers-recurring-false-absence-predictive-processing). Gemini's Dimension 4 also complains that predictive processing goes untested, but aims it at REBUS/psychedelics material the subject article does not contain (`predictive` 0, `REBUS` 0 — measured by the Gemini collect), so its half is **not** a second vote and was correctly declined. **Claude's half is article-level and real**: what is missing is PP-as-a-meta-problem-solution, a direct rival to the article's framing, not PP-as-an-account-of-transparency. Add at most a short engagement; this is an addition against 904 words of shared headroom on a file carrying five other open tasks.

### ✓ 2026-09-19: "realizationism follows from Bidirectional Interaction" over-derives, and the article owes a per-intuition-class account instead of one undifferentiated acquaintance appeal
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/meta-problem-of-consciousness.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-19, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. **Upgraded P2 → P1 by /combine-outer-reviews.** Two independent reviewers reached the same structural claim — that Bidirectional Interaction answers only *causal* independence and leaves modal/explanatory independence and the coincidence problem standing — and both grounded it in Chalmers 2020, *Debunking Arguments for Illusionism about Consciousness*. ChatGPT §1.7 enumerates the independence varieties; **Claude Opus 5 (Axis 1, Axis 3 item 1) calls the same gap "the single most important omission"** and quotes Chalmers 2020 verbatim: *"The realizationist can reasonably deny causal and constititive [sic] independence as described above … But there remains a case for a strong sort of modal/explanatory independence. In particular, there remains a near-complete structural explanation of the intuitions (in computational terms, say), and this explanation can obtain without consciousness."* and the coincidence premise: *"If there is a near-complete structural explanation of the intuitions that could obtain without consciousness, this gives a strong sense that if the intuitions are correct, their correctness is something of a coincidence."* **Those two spans were lifted by the Claude leg but were NOT re-verified against the primary this cycle** (that leg's own Verification Notes list Chalmers 2020 as not fetched); verify at `consc.net/papers/debunking.pdf` before quoting either in the article. The ChatGPT leg *did* fetch that paper and printed counts — `normative harmony` ×6, `phenomenal powers` ×31, `acquaintance` ×62 — so the paper's coverage of the vocabulary is established even though these two sentences are not. **Sequencing:** five other open tasks target this file (see the synthesis's queue-impact note); this one supplies the independence vocabulary the zombie-paragraph rewrite and the bibliography task both consume, so run it before or with them. ChatGPT 5.6 Pro §1.6, §1.7, §1.8, §3.1, §3.2, §3.4, improvements #3, #5, #6. **This is the ChatGPT review's deepest finding and the one it argues most carefully.** Interactionism entails that some conscious states make some physical difference; it does not by itself entail that consciousness realizes the processes producing problem intuitions, that the causal relation is truth-sensitive, or that the experience causes the judgment it *rationalizes*. Chalmers 2018 says in terms — verified verbatim this run — that "realizationism is also available to some reductionists", so realizationism is not a uniquely dualist prize either. (The reviewer's illustration of that point, "realizationist biological materialism and quantum materialism", is **overstated** — neither phrase occurs in either paper; the general claim survives, the example does not.) Two concrete asks. **(1) Distinguish the independence varieties.** Chalmers separates causal/constitutive, modal, and explanatory independence plus epistemic warrant; generic interactionist causation bears on the first and leaves the other three open. State which form Bidirectional Interaction actually challenges and which remain live. The reviewer's suggested conclusion is a fair, publishable narrowing: bidirectional interaction blocks the simplest causal-independence formulation *provided* consciousness contributes to the judgments in a content-sensitive way, with coincidence, modal independence and warrant still open. **(2) Split the four intuition classes.** The 2026-09-11 deep review correctly restored all four (explanatory, metaphysical, knowledge, modal), but the argument then treats acquaintance as vindicating them *en bloc*. Acquaintance with pain plausibly warrants "pain is occurring" and something of its character; it does not obviously warrant "pain is nonphysical", "no physical explanation could capture it", "a zombie is possible", or the nonphysical-fact reading of Mary. Give each class its own causal source and epistemic warrant. Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit. Identify what kind of engagement the issue calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft / deep-review changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. **Do not relitigate the calibration the article already gets right** — it correctly says its reply is in-framework and removes a defeater rather than adding evidence, and the reviewer explicitly credits that. The fix is narrowing an over-strong derivation, not re-opening a settled concession. Headroom: 904 words to hard.

### ✓ 2026-09-19: the zombie response in both meta-problem articles changes the meaning of "zombie" mid-argument, and the topic article promotes the equivocation to a "testable prediction"
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/metaproblem-of-consciousness-under-dualism.md
- **Notes**: **PART OF THE CYCLE'S 3/3 LOCUS CONVERGENCE.** All three 2026-09-19 reviewers independently marked the zombie/dissolution paragraph (`concepts/meta-problem-of-consciousness` L101-103) as the article's weakest passage, by three different routes — this task is the ChatGPT route (equivocation on "zombie"). The other two routes carry their own open P1s: the Claude route (the Map's own born-preserving commitment leaves no divergence to detect) and, folded into that same task, the Gemini route (circularity — the physicalist identity response is never engaged). **Discharge all three in one coordinated rewrite of the paragraph, not three sequential edits**, or each will strand the others' premises. ChatGPT 5.6 Pro §1.10, improvements #10 and #15. The section opens with the standard Chalmersian stipulation — a zombie is a physical/functional duplicate and therefore makes the same reports — and then answers that under interactionism the zombie's reports would differ and might show detectable divergence in philosophical depth or consistency. **Those cannot both hold under one use of "zombie":** if behaviour diverges, it was never the stipulated duplicate. The topic article sharpens the error into a *testable* zombie divergence, which compounds it, because an exact zombie's absence of consciousness is not independently measurable and any detected functional divergence disqualifies it as the duplicate. Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit. Identify what kind of engagement the issue calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft / deep-review changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. The reviewer's own reconstruction is a usable scaffold: an interactionist may argue (a) that exact zombies are metaphysically or nomologically **impossible**, (b) that a system starting in the same physical state but lacking mind would subsequently **diverge**, or (c) that a mindless emulator fails under richer tests. Only (a) is about philosophical zombies in the standard sense; (b) and (c) concern different counterfactual systems and should be named as such. The honest move is likely (a) plus an explicit note that (b)/(c) are a different claim — which also retires the "testable prediction" framing without conceding the philosophical point.

### ✓ 2026-09-19: four Chalmers attributions in the meta-problem cluster fail against the primary texts — "face-value solution", "noted this asymmetry", "entirely physical", and "in follow-up work"
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/meta-problem-of-consciousness.md

(1) **"face-value solution"** — `face-value` and `face value` occur **0 times** in the 2018 meta-problem paper and **0 times** in the 2020 debunking paper. Both this article and `topics/metaproblem-of-consciousness-under-dualism` present it as Chalmers's term; the topic article says so explicitly. Keep it as the Map's own label or drop it, but stop attributing it. (2) **"Chalmers himself noted this asymmetry"** — `asymmetry`/`asymmetric` occur **0 times** in either paper. The thought may be Chalmers-compatible; the attribution is not sourced. Recast as the Map's own inference unless a page-level citation can be supplied. (3) **"Chalmers frames the meta-problem as entirely physical"** drops Chalmers's antecedent. He also states the paper takes an **ecumenical** approach across the reductionist/non-reductionist divide. Restoring the conditional costs one clause and removes a misattribution of physicalism to Chalmers's framing. (4) **`topics/metaproblem-of-consciousness-under-dualism`: "In follow-up work, Chalmers developed realizationism" is simply false** — `realizationism` occurs **7 times** in the 2018 paper, which names and develops "meta-problem realizationism" as its third non-reductionist option. This one is a factual correction, not a calibration. **Both files have headroom** (2595/3500 and 2220/4000), so none of this is length-blocked. Per citation-ledger-ratifies-the-reading-not-just-the-metadata, a prior ledger pass certifying the *metadata* of these Chalmers entries does not certify these *readings*.

Content present in Hugo and **absent from source** means either (a) sync does not overwrite/delete in the way assumed, or (b) the sentence was removed from the obsidian sources and the Hugo copies were never regenerated. **Diagnose which before editing anything** — patching the two Hugo files by hand would paper over whichever mechanism produced them, and they would drift again.

A general check — compare `hugo/content/**` against freshly-synced output and report any file differing beyond the known link-conversion transforms — would settle whether this is an isolated pair or a class. Report the count; do not mass-edit.
  **CONTEXT:** found during the 2026-08-22 `^occams-razor-has-limits` → `^occams-limits` repair (commit `e8b4d102`), which fixed 6 occurrences across 5 **source** files (1 live article + 4 archive) and deliberately left these two alone as a different defect class.
  ⛔ **STOP — PREMISE REFUTED BY /replenish-queue 2026-08-22 (run 1001). DO NOT EXECUTE. There is no divergence and there is no broken link.** Every claim above was re-measured this run and each rests on a grep that could not see what it was looking for.
  **1. “Their obsidian sources contain 0 occurrences of `occams`” — CASE-AND-APOSTROPHE-BLIND GREP.** The sources spell it *Occam's*, not *occams*. `grep -n 'tenets#' obsidian/concepts/heterophenomenology.md` returns **L150**: `[[tenets#Occam's Razor Has Limits|Occam's Razor Has Limits tenet]]`, and the same heading-form wikilink is at `obsidian/topics/microphenomenological-interview-method.md` **L149**. Sync then slugifies the heading to `/tenets/#occams-razor-has-limits`. The Hugo copies are faithful translations of live source, not stale residue. Confirming the point from the other side: `git log -S 'occams-razor-has-limits' -- obsidian/concepts/heterophenomenology.md` returns **no commits**, because that literal string was never in the source — only its unslugified heading form was.
  **2. “The anchor target does not exist” — WRONG SURFACE, THEN QUOTE-MINIFICATION.** Hugo generates heading ids at *render* time, so grepping `hugo/content/tenets/_index.md` (markdown) for `id=` can never find them; only the explicit `<span id="occams-limits">` block anchors live in the markdown. In the built page the heading id is present: `grep -o '<h2[^>]*>' hugo/public/tenets/index.html` yields `<h2 id=occams-razor-has-limits>` alongside `dualism`, `minimal-quantum-interaction`, `bidirectional-interaction`, `no-many-worlds`. Note the id is emitted **unquoted** — so `grep -c 'id="occams-razor-has-limits"' hugo/public/tenets/index.html` also returns **0** and looks like confirmation. It is not. The link `/tenets/#occams-razor-has-limits` resolves to Tenet 5's heading and always did.
  **3. THE LOCUS COUNT WAS ALSO SHORT, IN THE ARCHIVE DIRECTION.** Counted with the exact string `(/tenets/#occams-razor-has-limits)`: **four** published pages carry it, not two — `hugo/content/concepts/heterophenomenology.md` (1), `hugo/content/topics/microphenomenological-interview-method.md` (1), `hugo/content/archive/concepts/microphenomenological-interview.md` (1) and `hugo/content/archive/topics/mysterianism-cognitive-closure.md` (3), plus `hugo/content/reviews/pessimistic-2026-04-23c.md` as review echo. All are correct links; the count is recorded only so a future sweep does not rediscover the archive pair and re-mint this.
  **WHAT REMAINS TRUE:** the 2026-08-22 repair (`e8b4d102`) was a real fix — the **block-anchor** spelling `#^occams-razor-has-limits` does not exist (the block anchor is `^occams-limits`). The **heading-form** link is a second, valid convention and must not be “fixed” to match. Recommend closing this entry as a false finding rather than executing it; kept at P3 and annotated per the 2026-08-18 precedent on the six-broken-research-links entry.
  ⛔ **PROMOTION ATTEMPTED AND WITHDRAWN BY /replenish-queue 2026-09-08 15:3xZ (run 1029) — THE 2026-08-22 REFUTATION ABOVE IS CORRECT AND THIS ENTRY STAYS AT P3. I briefly promoted it to P2 on a re-verification that reproduced the original defect, then caught the error and reverted the heading. Recording it so a fourth run does not repeat the same mistake.** The trap is **exactly** the one the 08-22 audit named, and it is easy to fall into twice: I searched both obsidian sources for `occams-razor-has-limits` (`str.find` → **-1**) and then, believing myself to be ruling out an emphasis- or wikilink-split form, searched case-insensitively for the bare stem `occams` — **also -1**, which read as decisive. **It is not: the apostrophe defeats it.** The sources spell it `Occam's`, so no search for `occams` can ever match, in any case-folding. Searching for `tenets#Occam` instead returns byte **22294** of `obsidian/concepts/heterophenomenology.md` and byte **25390** of `obsidian/topics/microphenomenological-interview-method.md`, both reading `[[tenets#Occam's Razor Has Limits|Occam's Razor Has Limits tenet]]`. **So the Hugo copies are faithful translations of live source; there is no stale-copy divergence and no broken link, precisely as run 1001 established.** Note that a "confirming" second search in the same character class is worth nothing — two greps that share a blind spot agree because they are blind, not because the claim is true (compounding-false-zeros-fabricate-a-defect, my-truncated-reads-manufacture-defects-and-false-accusations). **A SECOND PROCESS FAULT WORTH NAMING**: I read only the first 11 lines of this 19-line block before acting, so I never saw the ⛔ audit at all — the heading and opening Notes read as a clean, verified defect. **Whoever surveys this pool next: read each candidate block to its END before promoting it**, and scan for ⛔ / "NOT PROMOTED" / "PREMISE REFUTED" markers, which in this queue live at the BOTTOM of a block, after the original notes they overturn. **The 08-22 recommendation stands: close this as a false finding rather than executing it. Do not promote it again on the strength of a `grep occams` result.**

That note's central warning applies here and must be honoured: the literature reads these phenomena as cortical **release**, NOT as compensation or gap-filling, and the note documents five Map loci that currently over-read it as "compensatory" / "fills gaps". Do not import the compensation gloss into the auditory article. **WHAT TO ADD** (~400–700 words, well inside headroom; a new section alongside the existing three, or folded into §"Non-Spatial Pitch and Timbre Qualia"): (a) **tinnitus** as audition's percept-without-stimulus, with the same "this is a physicalist mechanism and the Map adopts it" concession the wing's other spokes make — cf. tactile on C-tactile afferents ("A dedicated affective fibre system is a *physicalist mechanism*") and chemosensory on the limbic bypass; (b) **amusia** as the sharpest of the three, because the article already argues that pitch and timbre are vividly determinate yet have no external object-property to be identified with, and amusia is the case where exactly those qualia go missing while hearing is otherwise intact; (c) **auditory agnosia** as audition's counterpart to visual-form agnosia, which would let this article connect to the two-streams material `concepts/capability-division-in-vision` already owns. **CALIBRATION IS NON-NEGOTIABLE**: the wing's shared spine is "accommodation, not proof" and this article already carries it in the "a proof" variant ("That is an accommodation, not a proof") plus "The Map does not claim audition settles dualism". Tenet alignment: Tenet 1 (Dualism) — a percept with no stimulus is the limiting case of the article's existing "the quality is not in the signal" argument; Tenet 3 (Bidirectional Interaction) — attentional modulation of tinnitus. Sync both trees (`obsidian/` AND `hugo/content/`). See `reviews/optimistic-2026-09-18-modality-interface-wing.md` §"Priority 1".
