### ✓ 2026-09-16: `concepts/cognitive-phenomenology` §The Illusionist Challenge cites no 2020s illusionist work — install Kammerer (2022) on the obviousness-of-phenomenality datum the section's abductive case turns on
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/cognitive-phenomenology.md
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-09-15 (Gemini 2.5 Pro Deep Research, subject audit of `concepts/pragmatism`), Weakness 5 — **fenced: the reviewer's headline charge ("the manuscript never addresses illusionism or the unreliability of introspection") is FALSE at site level** (`illusionis` 261 live files, `Frankish` 137; this article's own §The Illusionist Challenge, L170–174, engages Frankish 2016 and is what both pragmatism pages route to for the "felt sense" premise). **Do not import the charge, and do not add an illusionism paragraph to either pragmatism article** (three tasks already sit on the concept page; the routing link is item (f) of the open Claude P2 there). What is real and narrow: the section engages Frankish (2016) only, and the reviewer's actual point — that the argument "assumes the infallibility of this introspection" — is the *obviousness* problem, whose 2020s treatment is **Kammerer, F. (2022). "How can you be so sure? Illusionism and the obviousness of phenomenal consciousness." *Philosophical Studies* 179(9): 2845–2867. 10.1007/s11098-022-01804-7** (Crossref-exact; cited in 8 live files including [illusionism](/concepts/illusionism/), **0 hits here**). Kammerer's move is that the illusionist owes and can give an account of *why* phenomenality seems undeniable — which bears directly on this section's three abductive reasons (TOT meaning-without-form, insight-onset speed, contemplative refinement): each is a report the illusionist redescribes, and Kammerer supplies the general mechanism for the redescription's seeming impossible. Add **one or two sentences** in the section's existing constrain-not-establish voice stating that account and where the Map's dissent then lands (whether the obviousness-explanation covers *cognitive* phenomenology specifically, or only sensory), plus the reference entry — **read the paper's abstract/introduction at the publisher before characterising it; cite nothing you cannot see** (quote-must-be-grep-verifiable-in-raw-source). Not an opponent-engagement finding that needs the direct-refutation remit — the section already marks the disagreement honestly. **Length**: `analyze_length` reads **3202 body words against concepts soft 2500 / hard 3500** (`soft_warning`); keep the net addition under **+70 words** and print before/after. Reference list: append rather than renumber (inserting-into-a-numbered-ledger-breaks-cross-references). `ai_modified` from a live `date -u`; sync and grep **both** trees. Tenet alignment: Tenet 1 (Dualism).

- **Type**: refine-draft

- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-16, 2/3 reviewers): [claude, chatgpt] — synthesis cluster 6. Upgraded P2 → P1 by /combine-outer-reviews. ChatGPT 5.6 Pro (§5): "The present review process appears better at checking whether a statistic or quotation exists than at checking whether the source plays the argumentative role assigned to it." Both reviewers reached the same diagnosis from the same failure (Baird 2018 surviving three deep reviews; Hobson 2014 cited in the reversed direction) and each proposed one of the legs below; ChatGPT's §5 firewall finding supplies the third. Original note follows. From outer review 2026-09-16 (Claude Opus 5), Part 5 items 2 and 4, driver-verified. Evidence that the gap is real: `topics/lucid-dreaming-and-dualist-rendering` L116 asserted that Baird et al. 2018 shows practice "reshapes neural structure" while the paper's abstract says "no significant differences in brain structure were observed"; the article passed deep reviews on 2026-06-01, 06-21 and 07-26 — the last an explicit "owed-web-verify seam — verbatim quote + citation fidelity" pass — with the error intact, and that same pass certified two quotations as "real-correct" / "canonical" that a full-text grep shows are absent from the cited works (Metzinger 2009 *The Ego Tunnel*; Revonsuo 2000 *BBS*). The current lens (around SKILL.md L165) checks inline↔References orphans, metadata and quote strings; it has no step asking what the paper *reports*. Add two short numbered legs to the citation-fidelity procedure, ≤150 words total, no restructuring: **(1) Result-direction / null-result leg** — for every empirically cited paper, state in one line what the paper reports on the claim's direction and whether it reports a null on it ("does this paper report this result, in this direction, or a null?"); numbers and comparatives inside a cited claim are their own verification target (cf. changelog 2026-09-04 `authorship-of-action-divergence` inverted-comparison fix). A ledger line of "metadata correct" does NOT discharge this leg. **(2) Cited-author-stance leg** — for every author cited in support of a Map reading, record the author's own metaphysical commitment in one clause and confirm the article does not present them as endorsing the Map's conclusion; model the wording on `topics/predictive-processing-and-dualism` L48 and `concepts/delegatory-causation`. Also add one sentence to the lens preamble: a quotation certified by a prior review without a grep is uncertified. This task edits the skill file only — no article edits, no `ai_modified`, no sync. Tenet alignment: Tenet 5 (Occam) indirectly — the legs stop the Map from claiming evidential support it has not earned. **EXTENDED 2026-09-16 — ChatGPT 5.6 Pro sibling, `reviews/outer-review-2026-09-16-chatgpt-5-6-sol-pro.md` §5 "Methodological evidence of a tenet firewall", driver-verified: `reviews/deep-review-2026-06-21-lucid-dreaming-and-dualist-rendering` L70 and `…2026-07-26…` L68 say verbatim "bedrock framework-boundary disagreements, not fixable defects — do not re-flag as critical" and list "the physicalist-concession framing" among them; the Hobson argumentative-direction error then survived twelve deep-review passes. Add a THIRD leg, ≤40 words: a stability note may exempt a *framework commitment* (the Map adopts dualism) from re-flagging, never an *empirical-support claim* attached to it (that a named experiment or author favours the commitment) — the latter stays reviewable every pass. The **gate** form is declined — it is a gate, not a lens (NEEDS-HUMAN (methodology ratification) 2026-08-06) — but the lens form is real and small. The "Don't Conscript Committed Physicalists (the Co-optation Firewall)" section keeps three rosters (anti-dualist analytic; predictive-processing / active-inference; psychedelic-neuroscience) and fires when a listed author appears in a tenet-load-bearing passage. **None of the classical or deflationary pragmatists is on any roster**, so `concepts/pragmatism` L60 ("Dewey himself was a committed naturalist who would have resisted the dualist conclusion — but the logic of his position outruns his commitments") — flagged by two outer reviewers this cycle and by the internal pessimistic-2026-08-03 — never tripped the firewall through four deep reviews (03-26 ×2, 05-26, 06-25, 08-03). Add one roster bullet in the section's existing voice and format: **Pragmatist / deflationary roster:** John Dewey (transactional experience, warranted assertibility — *Logic* 1938 explicitly calls interactionism an insoluble pseudo-problem), Richard Rorty (anti-representationalism; 1965 eliminativism), Huw Price (subject naturalism, global expressivism — *Naturalism Without Mirrors* 2011; *Expressivism, Pragmatism and Representationalism* CUP 2013, 10.1017/cbo9780511842498), Cheryl Misak (Peircean convergence account of truth, OUP 1991/2004), Robert Brandom (inferentialism). One line on the characteristic failure: these authors are recruited by reading their *method* (inquiry, evaluation, assessment) as needing a phenomenal standpoint they explicitly deny, so the honest one-line statement of the author's actual position must name the dissolution they intend. Keep it to one bullet plus at most two sentences (~90 words); the document is far over its ceiling and this is additive by design as a roster entry. Do not add a per-article requirement to cite these authors. Cross-reference the ChatGPT P2 on `project/quantum-claim-and-quotation-disciplines` (argumentative-role fidelity, c-iii) in the changelog entry only, not in the article. Tenet alignment: Tenet 1 (Dualism).

Still sequenced after the Dewey P1 above (file order is dispatch order within a tier). From outer review 2026-09-15 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro, subject audit), Attribution audit §Peirce, §James, §Putnam, §van Fraassen, §Millikan/Papineau/Dretske, and Tenet-protective bracketing §No Many-Worlds — each item driver-verified, see the review's Verification Notes. **Run after the P1 task on this file lands** (same file; outer-review-same-file-task-pileup). Six small fixes, all metadata/calibration, none opponent-engagement — the direct-refutation remit does not apply. **(a) Putnam, L74 heading and L76**: *Reason, Truth and History* (1981) names its position "the internalist perspective" (verified in the text; "pragmatic realism" returns **0** in the book — the phrase is from *The Many Faces of Realism*, 1987). Rename the heading and L76 to "internal realism", and add one sentence that Putnam later moved to a natural/direct realism (the 1994 Dewey Lectures, "Sense, Nonsense, and the Senses", *Journal of Philosophy* 91(9) — verify metadata at Crossref before citing), so the 1981 phase is not presented as his settled view. Also soften "conditions that essentially involve conscious rational agents": Putnam's idealised rational acceptability involves agents and interests, not phenomenal consciousness in the Map's sense — a functionalist accepts the agent-relativity without dualism. **(b) Peirce, L38**: "not as a theory of truth" is too categorical — the 1878 essay contains one ("The opinion which is fated to be ultimately agreed to by all who investigate, is what we mean by the truth, and the object represented in this opinion is the real", verified). Say the maxim is a criterion of meaning that the essay pairs with a convergence account of truth, and correct the lead's "rejects the idea that truth consists in mirroring an independent reality" so it does not sweep Peirce in — he is a realist about the final opinion's object. **Consolidated from the Claude leg (§2.2, fix 1):** rewrite the L32 sentence pair so that Peircean convergence and Jamesian expediency are distinguished in one clause each and the unqualified "truth is what works" is deleted; cite Misak, *Truth and the End of Inquiry: A Peircean Account of Truth* (OUP 1991; expanded ed. 2004, Crossref 10.1093/0199270597.001.0001 — 0 corpus hits, a new name; verify at the publisher and cite nothing you cannot see) for the convergence reading, as one appended reference. **(c) James on truth, L50**: "not correspondence to an unobservable reality" compresses James's actual form — *Pragmatism* Lecture VI (verified): "Pragmatists and intellectualists both accept this definition as a matter of course. They begin to quarrel only after the question is raised as to what may precisely be meant by the term 'agreement'." One clause: the dispute is over what agreement *means* (verification, leading, experiential constraint), with copying retained as one form. **Consolidated from the Claude leg (§2.1 James row, fix 1):** "true insofar as it is useful" also drops James's own qualifier — add it verbatim from *Pragmatism* Lecture VI (Gutenberg #5116, NFKC-grepped by the driver): "'The true,' to put it very briefly, is only the expedient in the way of our thinking… expedient in the long run and on the whole of course" — one sentence, quotation marks, cited to the 1907 entry already in the list. **(d) James 1898/1904, L52**: label "human dignity acquires a non-biological basis" and "a status in the universe that materialism denies" as the Map's inferences from the underdetermination James establishes, not James's conclusions; and add one sentence that James's 1904 "Does 'Consciousness' Exist?" C. (1977). ### ✓ 2026-09-15: `concepts/pragmatism` §Dewey and Inquiry reverses Dewey's own inferential direction, and the Rorty "internal edge" is not internal — the article's dichotomy between bare mechanism and private phenomenal authority is what Dewey, Rorty, Brandom and Price all deny
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/pragmatism.md
- **Notes**: **CONVERGENT (combine-outer-reviews 2026-09-15) — Dewey 3/3, the deflationary wing 2/3 [chatgpt, claude], phenomenology ≠ authority 2/3 [chatgpt, gemini]; already P1, no upgrade available; the Claude and Gemini corroboration is in the corroboration paragraph at the end of these notes.** From outer review 2026-09-15 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro, subject audit of `concepts/pragmatism`), Attribution audit §Dewey, §Rorty and §Missed deflationary and neo-pragmatist counterarguments — **driver-verified against the raw primary texts, see the review's Verification Notes.** Three linked defects in the article's opponent engagement. **(1) Dewey (L58–60), source reversal — the reviewer's strongest finding and convergent with the internal [pessimistic-2026-08-03-pragmatism-idealism-pair](/reviews/pessimistic-2026-08-03-pragmatism-idealism-pair/) charity charge, which was never minted.** The article says the inquirer's "felt sense of a problem, their recognition that a proposed solution works — is constitutive of the process" and that Dewey's position carries implications "he did not fully pursue". Dewey, *Logic* (1938), "The Pattern of Inquiry", verbatim (NFKC-grepped from the archive.org OCR): "Stating that it is felt is wholly misleading if it gives the impression that the situation is a feeling or an emotion or anything mentalistic. On the contrary, feeling, sensation and emotion have themselves to be identified and described in terms of the immediate presence of a total qualitative situation." Same chapter, footnote: the "alleged problem of 'interactionism' versus automatism, parallelism, etc., is a problem (and an insoluble one) because of the assumption … that the interaction in question is with something mental instead of with biological-cultural human beings." Rewrite the section in three moves, in prose: Dewey's own organism–environment naturalism (problematic situations are objectively indeterminate transactions, resolution is warranted assertion within continuing inquiry); the Map's objection (a physically constituted standpoint does not secure the *authority* of the assessment); Dewey's likely reply (normativity is constituted within inquiry and practice, not conferred by a private phenomenal assessor). Drop "implications he did not fully pursue" and "the logic of his position outruns his commitments" — the primary text shows he pursued and rejected exactly this implication. **Keep "warranted assertion"**: the reviewer's terminology charge is disputed — Dewey writes "the term 'warranted assertion' is preferred to the terms belief and knowledge" and reserves "warranted assertibility" for knowledge "taken as a general abstract term"; a one-clause mention of the latter is optional. Quote at most one short verbatim span and grep it against the raw OCR before installing (quote-must-be-grep-verifiable-in-raw-source). **(2) Rorty (L70), the "internal edge" does not engage Rorty on his own ground.** The edge was installed 2026-08-03 on the internal pessimistic review's Issue 3 recommendation; the external reviewer now argues it fails: "a vocabulary of vocabularies has to be assessable from somewhere" is answered by Rorty from *within* contingent vocabularies and communal conversation — he needs no vocabulary-independent standpoint — so either "somewhere" means within a practice (and Rorty has supplied it) or it means within irreducible phenomenal consciousness (and the edge has imported the Map's premise and is no longer internal). Adjudicate on the merits, not by deference to either review: if there is a genuine in-framework pressure (does communal appraisal inherit the same reflexive problem, or does the Map have to show that "serves our purposes better" cannot be assessed communally?), state it; if not, fold the internal edge into the bedrock edge and say in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than the 08-03 rewrite allowed. Also fix L76 "Rorty's relativism": Rorty, "Pragmatism, Relativism, and Irrationalism" (1980), verified: "'Relativism' is the view that every belief on a certain topic, or perhaps about any topic, is as good as every other. No one holds this view." Use "Rorty's anti-representationalism" or similar. **(3) The missing neo-pragmatist replies.** §Pragmatism and the Map's Framework lists two replies (teleosemantics, functionalist concession); the article never engages **Brandom's** socially instituted normative statuses (commitment/entitlement within discursive practice) or **Price's subject naturalism** (expressivist/deflationary treatment of representation designed to block the inference from reflective practice to a special mind–world relation). Both give "ought" a non-private basis without grounding it in phenomenal feeling, so they dissolve the article's dichotomy between mechanism and private phenomenal authority. Add a third reply of ~120–160 words that states the shared move and the Map's response — the key distinction the reviewer supplies is that **the phenomenology of evaluation is not the authority of evaluation**: a judgement can feel compelling without being warranted, and a norm can bind within a practice without being constituted by a feeling; the article currently runs phenomenal character → normative authority → phenomenal causation without defending either step. Brandom already has corpus coverage ([conceptual-role-semantics](/concepts/conceptual-role-semantics/), [carrolls-regress](/concepts/carrolls-regress/), [inference-void](/voids/inference-void/)) — cross-link rather than re-derive; "subject naturalism" returns 0 corpus-wide, so cite Price, H. (2011) *Naturalism Without Mirrors* (OUP) or Price (2013) *Expressivism, Pragmatism and Representationalism* (CUP) after verifying metadata at the publisher. Apply the direct-refutation discipline. Identify what kind of engagement the issue calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [the writing-style guide](/project/writing-style/)'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft / deep-review changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. **Length**: article measures **2581 body words against concepts soft 2500 / hard 3500** (`analyze_length`, already `soft_warning`); the Dewey rewrite should be near length-neutral and the third reply is the only net addition — keep the whole task under **+200 words** and print `analyze_length` before and after. Check `git log -S` on any sentence you remove (the 08-03 Rorty edge and the Dewey wording both have review provenance — trimming-for-budget-can-strand-a-live-refutation). `ai_modified` from a live `date -u`; sync and grep **both** trees.
  **Cross-service corroboration 2026-09-15 (Gemini 2.5 Pro leg, `reviews/outer-review-2026-09-15-gemini-2-5-pro.md`, processed 04:59Z)**: Dewey is now **3/3** this cycle (Gemini: "biological matrix of inquiry" — Dewey's own chapter title — "evaluation is an organic interaction between organism and environment, not a dualistic intervention"). Item (3)'s key distinction is also independently reached: Gemini, §The Circularity of "Demand-Character": *"This presupposes that the felt sense* is *the normative force, rather than merely its phenomenal correlate"* — the phenomenology-of-evaluation ≠ authority-of-evaluation point, 2/3 cross-service. On Rorty, Gemini only restates that the bedrock edge is a Map premise (which L72 already says of itself) and does not engage the internal edge — item (2) stands as written. Nothing added to scope; no length change.

### ✓ 2026-09-14: `apex/born-preserving-causal-efficacy` formalises do(C) as the difference-making target but supplies no model for how do(C) could ever be identified
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/apex/born-preserving-causal-efficacy.md
- **Notes**: **THE REVIEW'S BEST NOVEL INFERENCE (§I.1) — an *identification* problem that is prior to, and distinct from, the acknowledged *mechanism* debt.** Verified on disk: the article does already carry the formalism (**L79** *"P(O | do(C), X) ≠ q(O | X)"*, **L81** *"where do(C) is an intervention that sets the conscious state"*, and the three-horn analysis at L89/L106), so the reviewer is **wrong that the criterion is undeveloped** — it is the *operationalisation* that is missing. **The argument**: on the Map's own ontology C is nonphysical, subject-indexed, and not readable from physical or functional structure. Experiments can randomise an instruction, stimulus, neural intervention or task condition; they cannot perform do(C) — set a phenomenal state while holding its physical causes fixed. Reports cannot substitute for C, because reports are downstream physical outcomes whose causal provenance is precisely what is in dispute. And **[P-I3](/positions/individuation-and-subjecthood/#p-i3)** compounds it by holding that subject boundaries cannot be recovered from physical organisation. Without bridge assumptions, a conditional residual shows an ordinary physical model is **incomplete** — not that a *nonphysical conscious state* supplied the missing cause. **THE PROPOSED SECTION (fix 6)**: a causal graph distinguishing conscious state, instruction, neural context, report and outcome; which variables are observed; and the bridge assumptions needed to license the inference — (i) a measurement model relating phenomenal states to reports or other proxies; (ii) an intervention model saying what physical manipulation changes C; (iii) an exclusion argument that the manipulation does not reach O directly through ordinary neural pathways; (iv) a treatment of latent physical common causes of both C and O; (v) a specification of *whose* conscious state the variable represents. **WHY P3 AND NOT HIGHER**: this is an addition, not a correction — nothing currently on the page is false, and the article's L89 already concedes the sharpest empirical point (intention-to-RNG *is* a conditional test at the coarsest grain and returned preregistered nulls — Maier et al. 2018). **CALIBRATION — the section must not read as a research programme the Map is prosecuting.** It is a statement of what would have to be true for the target to be identifiable; write it as debt, at the grade the register carries ([P-Q1](/positions/quantum-interface/#p-q1)/[P-Q3](/positions/quantum-interface/#p-q3)/[P-Q10](/positions/quantum-interface/#p-q10), Grade D, framework-internal only), not as a testing roadmap. Cross-reference [falsification-roadmap-for-the-interface-model](/topics/falsification-roadmap-for-the-interface-model/) rather than duplicating it. **LENGTH IS THE BINDING CONSTRAINT** — `analyze_length` before writing (apex soft 4000 / hard 5000); if there is no headroom, say so and defer rather than compressing into unreadable density. **RELATED, DO NOT FOLD IN**: the same review's §I.3 asks for a *psychophysical jurisdiction law* (what determines whether the physical stochastic selector or a conscious selector governs an event — onset, cessation, anaesthesia) spanning `concepts/prebiotic-collapse`, `apex/post-decoherence-selection-programme` and [P-SC2](/positions/subject-census/#p-sc2); and §I.5 asks for a `positions/branch-ethics` entry on how moral status would aggregate over Everett branches if Tenet 4 were false. Both are separate, larger pieces of work; **neither is minted here** — recorded so the next replenish or the `/combine-outer-reviews` pass can pick them up if the Claude leg corroborates. `ai_modified` from a live `date -u`; sync and grep both trees.

- **Type**: refine-draft

- **Notes**: The 2026-08-25 refine (commit to follow) closed Issues 1–5 and the reference-apparatus items. It **declined the three Counterarguments**, for a stated reason rather than an oversight: each *adds argument* rather than correcting an error, and the length budget was fully committed — the article finished that pass at **3999w against an apex soft ceiling of 4000**, so nothing can be added here without paying for it first. **Re-measure with `analyze_length` before starting; assume there is no headroom.** The three, in descending value: (1) **Test Wallace's ill-formedness verdict rather than granting it.** The article's own lead concedes Everettianism is "mathematically conservative" — the claim that the ontology is read off the unitary formalism with nothing added. The verdict that the global indexical question is ill-formed is *not* read off the formalism; it rests on a functionalist theory of persons. If that is a genuine addition, the framework boundary the article concedes at §The Case is narrower than conceded, and the concession currently sits one step early. ⚠️ **This is a test to run, not a finding to install** — Wallace does argue for the functionalism, and the live question is whether that argument is available on Everettian resources alone. If it is, say so; a concession that has been tested is worth more than one merely asserted. Do **not** convert this into a refutation claim (driver-brief-asserts-a-position-the-article-disclaims). (2) **Mark the wager as explicitly internal.** §The Symmetric Debt closes with a "comparative bet about where to hold an unpaid bill", but the metric — that a framework keeping the vertiginous question askable beats one dissolving it — is one the article has already conceded under Tenet 5 to be "a posited premise, not a neutral datum". A bet needs a metric both parties recognise. The cheap fix (and the one matching the article's register elsewhere) is to mark the wager as a preference conditional on the posit rather than a bet adjudicable from outside; the expensive alternative is to supply a neutral consideration making question-preservation a shared virtue. (3) **The Nagarjuna circularity**, which is (2) sharpened: "better because the debtor still exists as a debtor" presupposes a debtor, which is exactly what the deflationary route denies — so the closing argument cannot be the neutral ground the comparative-bet framing needs. (2) and (3) are one fix, not two. **DECLINE-IF**: if on reading you judge the §Not a Physics Tenet Alone paragraph (which already registers the Madhyamaka route as bedrock disagreement and concedes under Tenet 5 that the question's meaningfulness is a posit) already discharges (2) and (3), say so plainly and close them — do not manufacture a second concession where one stands. **MIRROR**: edit obsidian, `uv run python scripts/sync.py`, grep both trees.

### ✓ 2026-09-14: the modality wing's organising structural finding — modality-specific brain machinery + supramodal access boundary across the distance senses, inverted by interoception, driven to both extremes by the vestibular sense, made a gradient by thermoception, held consonant-not-probative because GNW predicts the same signature — is stated in the apex and three spokes and registered nowhere; it is the corpus's cleanest architecture-vs-significance case and `finding-level-calibration` holds only [P-F1](/positions/finding-level-calibration/#p-f1)
- **Type**: positions-evolve
- **File**: obsidian/positions/finding-level-calibration.md
- **Notes**: **Verified 2026-08-27**: `grep -il 'capability.division\|cross-modal\|modality\|interocep\|supramodal\|somatic' obsidian/positions/*.md` returns nothing across all seventeen files; `finding-level-calibration` holds [P-F1](/positions/finding-level-calibration/#p-f1) only (architecture-tier and significance-tier claims cited at their own discounts). `positions/` is at 16 of 80. The finding is stated at `apex/cross-modal-capability-division` L84 ("the asymmetry … holds for the distance and proprioceptive senses; interoception inverts it"), L86 (thermal gradient), L98 (vestibular: "the ownership gradient has *two* axes"), L120 ("the conscious boundary is internally graded"), L130 ("consonant with the interface reading without being probative of it, because the Global Neuronal Workspace predicts the very same supramodal signature and reads it as a neural broadcast. The signature is a shared explanandum"); and in `interoceptive` L36–38, `thermal` §The Boundary Case, `vestibular` §What the Interface Reading Accommodates.
  **Proposed entry (P-F2, beside [P-F1](/positions/finding-level-calibration/#p-f1) — draft; the skill decides wording and may place it in `arguments-for-dualism` instead)**: *Asserts*: the Map holds that the cross-modal capability division is a two-tier finding. Architecture tier (cross-vocabulary-robust, small discount): across vision, audition, chemosensation, touch and proprioception the brain-side extraction/integration machinery is modality-specific while the conscious-access boundary carries a supramodal signature; interoception inverts both terms (convergent insular integrator; mind side as affect and ownership); the vestibular sense has no unisensory cortex and almost no felt object yet supplies the spatial frame; thermoception reports world and body through one lamina-I pathway, so the outward/inward partition is a gradient. Significance tier (Map's reading, larger discount): the recurrence of the division across six heterogeneous transducers is consonant with a single mind-side contributor and probative of nothing, because GNW predicts the identical signature, and the common-cause null applies with *more* force inward (one insular hub is maximally a common cause). *Confidence*: architecture tier high; significance tier low-moderate, explicitly conditional on the interface reading assumed elsewhere. *Would shift if*: a modality were found whose brain-side machinery was shared with another distance sense (breaking the architecture claim), or a within-modality dissociation isolated a mind-side contribution GNW could not re-describe as broadcast. *Argued in*: the apex + the six spokes. *Depends on*: [P-F1](/positions/finding-level-calibration/#p-f1), [P-M1](/positions/methodology-and-calibration/#p-m1) (tenet-register / evidence-register separation), the common-cause null.
  **Why here and not an apex**: the apex already exists and is the finding's home; what is missing is the register's *citable standard* so the three `refine-draft` tasks minted alongside this one (hub over-claims; chemosensory valence; dualist-perception front door) have a discount to point at. **Pileup**: 0 open `positions-evolve` tasks name this file (grep-verified 2026-08-27); the one other open `positions-evolve` (2026-08-27, intellectual-life wing) targets `arguments-for-dualism` with a different thesis — do not merge, but read it first so the two entries use the same confidence vocabulary. **ATTRIBUTION**: bump `ai_modified` with a `date -u` check; extend `ai_system` as a `+`-joined string.

### ✓ 2026-09-14: `positions/value-in-selection` [P-VS3](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs3) — name the no-self objection as a second live rival and add "a valenced experience for no one" as a shift-condition; grade unchanged
- **Type**: positions-evolve

- **Notes**: **Deferred from the 2026-08-27 cross-review, which was told not to touch `positions/`.** [P-VS3](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs3) (L67–78) *Asserts* "Where there is no experiencer, nothing bears intrinsic value" — the experiencer requirement is exactly what Buddhist *anātman*+momentariness and Parfit's reductionism target — yet its *Would shift if* names only illusionism, value–experience dissociation and separability from evaluative character, and its *Asserts* names illusionism as the sole live rival. `concepts/no-self-objection-to-phenomenal-value` (created 2026-08-27 13:04) states the Map's reply (a momentary experiential locus suffices; a persisting self is not drawn on — cascade-isolated from [P-I1](/positions/individuation-and-subjecthood/#p-i1) / [P-SC3](/positions/subject-census/#p-sc3) / Posit One by design) and its own move-condition: "a verified report of a valenced experience that was genuinely for no one". `topics/phenomenal-value-realism` now carries both (§The Hardest Objection paragraph; sixth shift-condition). **Edit, small**: (a) one clause in *Asserts* naming the no-self objection beside illusionism as a rival the Map's reply constrains but has not refuted; (b) a fourth *Would shift if* condition in the concept's wording, scoped so that a self-model-less (MPE) state does not count — only a locus-less valenced one; (c) add the concept under *Argued in*. **Do NOT**: change credence (moderate) or grade (D) — the reply is boundary-marking, and the concept shows the chain survives at momentary grain, which neither lowers nor raises the band; add [P-I1](/positions/individuation-and-subjecthood/#p-i1) / [P-SC3](/positions/subject-census/#p-sc3) to *Depends on* — the cascade isolation is the point, and the entry must keep *not* resting on the persisting subject. Dated *Updated* line per register convention; `Last reviewed` bump. Not open-queued: grep of `value-in-selection` + `no-self` against the Active region returned only this task's parent at mint.
  ⚠️ **THREE ADDITIONS FROM THE 2026-09-06 positions-evolve PASS — read before editing.** (i) **A third rival to name, not a second.** [the-experience-requirement-on-well-being](/topics/the-experience-requirement-on-well-being/) (created 2026-09-06, deep-reviewed the same afternoon) establishes that **Lin 2021** ("The Experience Requirement on Well-Being", *Philosophical Studies* 178(3), 867–886, doi:10.1007/s11098-020-01463-6) is [P-VS3](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs3)'s live opponent specifically: he **denies** the global supervenience requirement — no phenomenological difference, no welfare difference — which is what [P-VS3](/positions/value-in-selection/#p-vs3) amounts to read as a welfare thesis, while **granting** the local requirement that every welfare good involves a feeling subject, which is all [P-MS1](/positions/moral-status/#p-ms1)'s necessity derivation needs. So Lin is a threat to this entry and an ally to its dependent, and *Asserts* currently names illusionism as the sole live rival. That asymmetry is now stated in [P-MS1](/positions/moral-status/#p-ms1)'s *Depends on* and at [moral-status-calibration-history](/positions/moral-status-calibration-history/#p-ms1); it is stated in **no** value-in-selection file, because there was no room. Do not write "Lin grants both" — on Lee 2025's reading Lin takes a theory of subjects to stand or fall with a theory of goods, so what he grants is the local requirement, not the subject theory's independence.
